Dressing him, Qinggua prattled on: "Always hoped Young Master would wed a Young Madam to keep you in line—never guessed it'd be Miss Zhu, with her bad name and a divorce behind her! Wonder what Master and Madam were thinking."
For the very first time, Chen Haoyu praised Qinggua: "Hit the nail on the head! Even you see it's crazy, yet my parents got hoodwinked by the Prefect into dumping his unwanted daughter on me—how miserable is my luck?!"
Thinking harder, alarm lit Qinggua's face: "Wait—the Prefect hauling you home in ropes... don't tell me you forced yourself on Miss Zhu outside, got caught red-handed, and got strong-armed into marrying her?! That why Master and Madam caved?!"
Fully dressed, Chen Haoyu spun and aimed a kick at Qinggua's shin: "Shut your trap! Sure I love pretty girls, yet molesting a tigress?! Not in a million years!"
Dodging half clear so the kick barely grazed him, Qinggua kept running his mouth: "You really do need a tough Young Madam to rein you in, Young Master—otherwise all you do is bully me!"
Chen Haoyu swung another kick: "So what if I bully you? Bullying you means I like you—plenty of folks beg to get bullied by me and don't make the cut!"
Qinggua hopped clear again, leaving Chen Haoyu too lazy to chase.
Flashing a cheeky grin, Qinggua said, "Better skip liking me then, Young Master."
Chen Haoyu snapped cool: "Fine—stop following me from now on."
"No way! Go ahead and kick me, Young Master!" Qinggua stuck his rear out.
Chen Haoyu cracked a laugh: "What a shameless little bootlicker—not even worth my foot! Come on, you're coming out with me."
Qinggua's eyes lit up like lanterns: "Did I hear right, Young Master?! Taking me outside?!"
"Don't want to go? Fine, I'm off." Chen Haoyu started for the door.
Scrambling after with a huge grin: "My honor to tag along, Young Master! Heard rolling with you means non-stop gourmet feasts and high living!"
"You bet! Stay loyal to me and I'll treat you to brothels, gambling dens, fine food—pure paradise!" Chen Haoyu boasted as they walked out.
Excited, Qinggua bounced: "Never even seen inside a brothel—when are you taking me, Young Master?"
"Right after we handle urgent business! Go hitch a carriage—we're swinging by Wangyue Pavilion first, and once this big mess is sorted, straight to Chunxiang Brothel for fun!"
Chen Haoyu had done the math: guilty over his rumor stunt, his parents wouldn't dare defy the Prefect; sheltered inside Chen Manor, Grandmother thought this match was heaven-sent—nobody inside Chen Manor could bail him out.
His best weapon was Zhu Shuzhen herself! With her volcanic temper, tell her about the betrothal and she'd blow the roof off Zhu Manor—killing the engagement without him lifting a finger!
Thrilled just to step past the gate, Qinggua asked, "What's Wangyue Pavilion, Young Master?"
"Quit yapping and follow orders!"
Outside Chen Manor, Qinggua had the stablehands hitch up a carriage and took the reins himself to drive his master over.
Guided by Chen Haoyu, Qinggua located Wangyue Pavilion fast, pulled up, waited for his master to step down, and went to tell a gate boy: "Young Master Chen is calling—go announce him."
"Wait a moment; I'll report."
Reaching the bedchamber courtyard, the boy called through: "Young Lady, a Young Master Chen is at the gate asking to see you."
Fresh from drilling her servants in Qianli Fist and just catching her flow on a new huaben chapter, Qianli Xun guessed "Young Master Chen" meant Chen Haoyu—nothing good ever followed him!
Refusing to budge, she kept right on writing.
Reading her mistress's mood, Kuqiao lifted the curtain and told the boy: "Tell Young Master Chen Miss has no time today."
Trotting back to the gate, the boy relayed: "My mistress is busy today and can't see Young Master Chen."
Chen Haoyu bristled: "Think I'm dying to see HER?! Go tell Zhu Shuzhen if she doesn't want to marry me, she'd better drag herself out here right now!"
Mystified yet scared to ask, the boy sprinted back to whisper outside her door: "Young Lady, Young Master Chen says if you don't want to marry him, come out and see him right now."
Alarm flashing with suspicion, Qianli Xun set down her brush and stood: "Come on, Kuqiao—let's see what Chen Haoyu's up to."
Draping a cape over her shoulders, Kuqiao followed her out to the gate.
Taking one look at Chen Haoyu's punchable smirk, Qianli Xun snapped, "What do you want?! Spit it out and get moving!"
Stepping across the threshold, Chen Haoyu sneered: "That sly old fox father of yours couldn't offload his unwanted daughter, so he strong-armed my parents into proposing! In seven days our betrothal gets sealed! I don't want to marry you, and you sure don't want to marry me—march straight back to Zhu Manor and talk your dad out of marrying you to the Chens!"
Stunned in disbelief, Qianli Xun stared: "Are you pulling my leg?! How on earth could something that insane happen?!"
"Look like I've got time to joke about my own wedding?! Go ask your dad yourself! Marrying you—ugh, gives me goosebumps just picturing it! Here's the deal: you work on your dad by any means necessary, I work on my parents and grandmother. Pull this off, and I swear I'll stay out of your lane for life—spot you on the street and I'll bolt on sight! Deal?"
Watching Chen Haoyu's dead-serious panic, she knew he wasn't joking—nobody joked about his own forced wedding! Eighty percent chance it was real.
"I'll verify this myself. If you're telling the truth—do you honestly think I'd say yes to marrying YOU?!" Turning sharp, she tossed back: "No thanks, and never darken my door again!"
Chen Haoyu yelled after her: "Bit early to say 'never again'!"
Turning away with a smug grin, he patted himself on the back: "Pure genius! Unleash her on old Zhu, and this betrothal is toast!"
Clapping Qinggua's shoulder: "Come on! Taking you to Chunxiang Brothel to celebrate—no more holding back, time to break my fast!"
Qinggua cheered: "Woohoo! Rolling with Young Master rocks!"
Meanwhile, after parting from Zhu Xiyan, Lin Jue had ridden straight to Youlin Retreat to report the culprit to Prince Yi.
Shen Jianchen never guessed Chen Haoyu was the mastermind behind the smears—as far as he knew, Chen Haoyu and Qianli Xun barely crossed paths!
Then that glimpse at Zhu Manor's banquet clicked back into memory: those two had bad blood from way back.
Even without dealing with Chen Haoyu directly, one run-in at Juxiang Tower had shown him the kid was an incorrigible rake—a smear campaign fit his playbook to a tee.
Hands-off though he stayed in local politics, Shen Jianchen knew Lin'an's balance of power: the Chen clan held no offices yet controlled half the city's lifeblood.
Plus the Chens and Zhus were in-laws—explaining why Zhu Xiyan handled it behind closed doors to keep things quiet.
Weighing the Chens' clout, Shen Jianchen agreed dragging it into court made no sense.
"Fast work, Commandant Lin—nabbing the mastermind this quick proves real skill."
Glowing with visions of another promotion, Lin Jue bowed: "Your Highness flatters me—serving Prince Yi is my greatest honor."
Shen Jianchen nodded and asked, "How many people know?"
"Only Your Highness, the Prefect, myself, and a handful of my most loyal men—every one sworn to silence."
"Head back for now; let me think this over."
Once Lin Jue bowed out, Shen Jianchen decided there was nothing to think over—bully the woman he loved, and only one word applied: BEAT!
"Bai Feng!"
Stepping in from outside: "Your orders, Your Highness?"
"Go grab Chen Haoyu and haul him beneath the Divine Willow—I'm teaching him a lesson."
A royal order to beat someone up thrilled Bai Feng—he'd been drilling punches for months without a live punching bag! Chen Haoyu made great practice!
"Yes, Your Highness! Bringing him right back!"
Bolting out, Bai Feng drove into town, swung past Chen Manor, and learned from gatekeepers Second Young Master wasn't home.
Where did a rake like Chen Haoyu go? Either a brothel or a gambling den! Having been to Chunxiang Brothel before, he checked there first.
Sure enough, pulling up near Chunxiang Brothel, he caught Chen Haoyu stepping straight out of his carriage! Launching his light-foot technique at blinding speed, Bai Feng swooped in like lightning and snatched Chen Haoyu clean off his feet...
Recently coached in light-foot work by Prince Yi on top of his boxing drills, Bai Feng had leveled up fast.
Blinded by a sudden blast of wind, Qinggua squeezed his eyes shut—opening them to empty air where his master had stood! Whipping his head round, he saw his master tossed into a strange carriage that tore off down the lane before he could even yell!
Qinggua collapsed onto his backside in panic: "What do I do?! How do I face Master and Madam?!"
Inside the carriage, struck on his acupoints, Chen Haoyu sat frozen stiff and mute.
He recognized Bai Feng as Prince Yi's guard—meaning Prince Yi had snatched him! Having zero grudge with Prince Yi, why on earth was he kidnapped?!
At least Prince Yi wouldn't kill him—everyone swore Prince Yi was kindhearted.
Riddled with confusion, he was driven straight out past the city gates to the foot of the hills.
Stopping the carriage, Bai Feng tied Chen Haoyu's wrists first, then tapped open his acupoints.
"Out you get, Young Master Chen."
Finding his voice, Chen Haoyu barked, "By what right do you kidnap me?!"
Bai Feng grinned: "By His Highness's order! Move it—His Highness is waiting beneath the Divine Willow."
Climbing down in foul disgust—kidnapped TWICE in one day, talk about cursed luck!—he grumbled as he walked: "If a royal prince summons me, wouldn't I come on my own? Why tie me up?!"
Bai Feng sneered: "Quit yapping—you're about to get a taste of what's coming!"
Marching Chen Haoyu along the base of the hills toward the Divine Willow, melted snow left bare winter earth all around.
Chen Haoyu had never set foot out here before, having heard endless talk of young lovers tying red silk ribbons to some magic willow—total nonsense, he figured.
Up close, aside from a thick trunk and old age, it looked like any plain willow.
Standing beneath the branches as Bai Feng marched Chen Haoyu up, Shen Jianchen skipped waiting for bows and barked: "Untie him, Bai Feng, and beat him with your bare fists—just avoid anything fatal."
Without a second thought, Bai Feng slipped the ropes off Chen Haoyu.
Panic seized Chen Haoyu: "Prince Yi! Prince Yi! Royal rank or not, you can't beat people for nothing—"
Before he finished the sentence, Bai Feng's fists and boots rained down in a relentless storm...
#CH 143
#TITLE: Returning to Zhu Manor to Check on the Betrothal
True to Prince Yi's training, Bai Feng dodged every vital spot while pounding Chen Haoyu black, blue, and swollen from head to toe—a total wreck!
His temper cooled at last, Shen Jianchen called off the beating: "Enough—keep going and you'll cripple him. Just make sure he remembers crossing me doesn't end well. Open your eyes wider next time, watch who you mess with, and don't step on a tiger's tail."
Lowering his fists, Bai Feng beamed—his first time pummeling a rich dandy, and it felt fantastic!
Sprawled groaning on the frozen dirt, aching in every bone, Chen Haoyu still had zero clue how he had offended Prince Yi.
Gasping through swollen lips, he asked, "I've got... no grudge... with Prince Yi... Why have your man... beat me?!"
Stepping close, Shen Jianchen looked down cold: "Think back on the filthy stunts you pulled lately and who you bullied—you'll figure it out."
The rumor campaign flashed into Chen Haoyu's head: "Because... I spread... rumors about... Zhu Shuzhen?!"
"Not as dumb as you look—bull's-eye."
Careful not to let Chen Haoyu suspect his own feelings for Qianli Xun and risk hurting her, Shen Jianchen added a cover: "Because of your smears against Miss Zhu, Wenyuan lost the woman he loves and suffered hell—beating you is getting off light!"
"So... Your Highness beat me... for Qiao Wenxian... Thought it was... for Zhu Shuzhen..."

"Relax... kill me... and I still won't... brag about... something this humiliating..."

Prince Yi strode off with Bai Feng, leaving Chen Haoyu flat on the freezing ground. Shivering against the cold earth, he gritted his teeth and fought to stand—collapsing three or four times before dragging himself upright and hobbling forward, every limb heavy as lead.
Stumbling on until twilight darkened the hills, he finally reached the road back toward town—tripping over a stone and crashing down without even feeling the fresh scrape over the symphony of pain already screaming across his body.
Crawling back up, he limped onward, swallowing a choke of bitter rage he dared neither speak aloud nor avenge.
"Young Master! Found you at last!"
Qinggua's voice rang out of the dusk—making Qinggua look downright angelic to Chen Haoyu for the first time in his life!
After watching his master snatched off the street, Qinggua had sat stunned for half a minute before snapping out of it, hopping into the driver's seat, and whipping the horses in pursuit.
Betting the kidnappers headed outside the city gates, he had driven straight out—uncertain which road they took, searching trail after trail until reaching the foot of the hills.
With dusk closing fast and hope nearly gone, he spotted a limping silhouette in the gloom—and sure enough, it was his master!
At the sight of Qinggua, Chen Haoyu's last scrap of grit snapped and he pitched forward.
Qinggua lunged to catch him: "Who had the nerve to beat you like this, Young Master?!"
"Quit... asking... Take me to... Yongning... Residence... Won't go home... to Chen Manor... till I heal..."
Relieved at least to have found his master alive so he wouldn't face Master's wrath empty-handed, Qinggua hoisted him into the carriage and drove back into town to Yongning Residence.
Helping him into bed at Yongning Residence, Qinggua sent servants running for a physician, who prescribed both herbal drafts and topical salves.
Once maids smoothed cooling ointment across his bruises, relief eased his groans. Lying face-down on the mattress, he called, "Qinggua, come here."
Stepping to the bedside: "Yes, Young Master?"
"Go back to Chen Manor and tell Father I'm staying at Yongning Residence for a spell. Not a word about today."
Promising he would, Qinggua bristled: "The second you heal, Young Master, we're going to hunt down whoever beat you and pay them back!"
"Shut it—never mention today again."
Settling back onto the pillows, Chen Haoyu savored the cool sting of the salve—never in his life had plain comfort felt this sweet.
Not daring another peep, Qinggua rode back to Chen Manor and relayed his master's message word for word to Chen Jianglin.
Exasperated at his son running off again yet helpless to stop him, Chen Jianglin cheered right up thinking of the Zhu betrothal—eager to welcome Miss Zhu into Chen Manor to whip his boy into shape.
Meanwhile, after Chen Haoyu left Wangyue Pavilion, Qianli Xun could not shake the dread that this insane betrothal might actually be real, deciding to go straight to Zhu Manor for the truth.
It was her first time setting foot back inside Zhu Manor since being driven out—after all, why return to a house that threw her out?
Yet if Zhu Xiyan was arranging her marriage again, he still counted her as his daughter—giving her every right to demand answers!
Arriving at Zhu Manor, she skipped having servants announce her and walked straight to Madam Xiang's quarters.
Dozing on her couch, Madam Xiang opened her eyes at the call of "Mother" to find her daughter standing before her.
Sitting up at once: "You're back, Shuzhen? Did your father summon you?"
Skipping pleasantries, Qianli Xun cut straight in: "Mother, do you know anything about Father betrothing me to the Chen family?"
Madam Xiang blinked in genuine surprise: "Betrothing you to the Chen family? Never heard a word! Where did you pick that up?"
Her mother's bewildered face eased the tight spring in Qianli Xun's chest—had Chen Haoyu been messing with her?
Sitting beside Madam Xiang, she explained, "Chen Haoyu showed up at Wangyue Pavilion today claiming our two families are getting engaged—it sounded so wild I came straight here to check."
"Young Master Chen said that? Your father hasn't breathed a word to me!" Unease flickered in Madam Xiang lest her husband was hiding something.
"Mother, could you go ask Father for me?"
"Of course—come along with me and see your father too."
Fearing two sentences with Zhu Xiyan would spark another shouting match, Qianli Xun shook her head: "Better I wait here in Mother's room so Father doesn't yell at me for stepping back into Zhu Manor."
"Wait right here then; Mother will get to the bottom of it."
Taking a maid, Madam Xiang headed to Zhu Xiyan's study, finding him reading a book in peace.
Rumors crushed, a rich marriage lined up for his daughter, Zhu honor restored—Zhu Xiyan hadn't felt this chipper in months!
He even fancied Chen Haoyu's smear campaign had been heaven-sent; without it, what leverage would he ever have had to make the Chens take his daughter?
Determined to keep the betrothal under wraps until sealed—knowing Shuzhen would raise hell and wreck it if wind reached her—he hadn't even breathed a word to Madam Xiang.
Afraid his daughter-in-law Chen Wenxin might visit Chen Manor and spill the beans to Shuzhen, the minute Zhu Xiyan got home from Chen Manor he had summoned Zhu Jingyuan and loaded him up with a tall stack of books for Chen Wenxin to copy out in her neat hand.
Mystified, Zhu Jingyuan had carried them over anyway.
Copying that stack would tie Chen Wenxin down for two weeks—zero free hours to visit Chen Manor or hear about the engagement!
He had also ordered gatekeepers to bar Chen Haoyu from setting foot inside Zhu Manor.
With every loophole sealed, all left was waiting for the Chens to arrive with betrothal gifts.
Stepping into the study, Madam Xiang announced, "Husband, Shuzhen came back..."
"What's she doing back here?" Without setting down his book, Zhu Xiyan put on a frosty scowl.
"She came asking whether the Chen family is coming to propose."
Lowering his book, sharp alertness flashed in Zhu Xiyan's eyes: "Has Madam heard anything about that?"
Madam Xiang shook her head: "Not a word—Young Master Chen told Shuzhen himself."
"That boy is a wild prankster—how can anyone trust a word out of his mouth?" Lifting his book again, Zhu Xiyan tested casually, "If it were true, would Madam want Shuzhen marrying into the Chen family?"
"If it were true, it wouldn't be bad at all—only Shuzhen would probably fight it. Wenxin told me Shuzhen and her brother clash like cats and dogs, ready to throw punches the second they meet."
Pleased his secrecy had paid off, Zhu Xiyan waved a hand: "Go tell Shuzhen a Chen betrothal is pure hot air—send her back and tell her not to wander into Zhu Manor without cause."
"Yes, Husband."
Returning to her chamber, Madam Xiang passed the denial straight to her daughter.
Still half skeptical—even a reckless dandy like Chen Haoyu wouldn't joke about his own wedding!—Qianli Xun decided to double-check with her sister-in-law.
Walking over to her brother's courtyard, she found Chen Wenxin buried at her desk writing nonstop, beckoning Shuzhen to sit without lifting her brush.
"What keeps Sister-in-law writing so furiously?"
"Sigh! Out of nowhere Father-in-law dumped this mountain of books on me to copy! Look at this stack—can't even visit you at Wangyue Pavilion for weeks, so don't hold it against me!"
"How could I? Where's my brother?"
"Always inseparable—asking for your brother the second you walk in! Just stepped out on errands; too buried in copying to ask where."
"Say, Sister-in-law—have you heard a certain piece of news?"
"What news?" Chen Wenxin asked, brush still flying.
"Chen Haoyu showed up at Wangyue Pavilion today claiming the Chens and Zhus are getting engaged..."
Her brush froze mid-stroke as Chen Wenxin looked up beaming: "Is there really news that wonderful?!"
Irritation spiked in Qianli Xun: "Looks like you know nothing—meaning that brother of yours made the whole thing up just to mess with me! Wait till I get my hands on him!"
Resuming her copying, Chen Wenxin grinned: "Maybe Haoyu actually likes you and cooked that up to test your reaction!"
"Now Sister-in-law's talking crazy! Chen Haoyu liking me?! If he ever likes me I'll jump straight into the Qiantang River!"
"Is my brother really that awful?"
"Worse than awful!"
Unbothered, Chen Wenxin kept writing: "Relax—I haven't heard a syllable about any betrothal. Next time I catch Haoyu, I'll box his ears for teasing you with nonsense like that."
Weight lifted off her shoulders at last, Qianli Xun smiled: "Hearing Sister-in-law say that puts my mind at rest. I'll head back to Wangyue Pavilion before Father spots me and blows his top."
"Too swamped to see you out—I'll visit Wangyue Pavilion once I dig out from under these books."
Smiling goodbye, Qianli Xun rose and left.
Riding home in the carriage, Xiaguo wondered aloud, "Why would Young Master Chen lie to you about something like that, Miss?"
"Who knows what rattles inside his skull? Better hope he doesn't cross my path again, or one punch puts him flat on the pavement."
Beside her, Kuqiao hid a quiet giggle.
Qianli Xun raised a brow: "What are you snickering at?"
Kuqiao beamed: "Miss sounds just like her old self again—makes me happy!"
Qianli Xun fell silent—not from fresh agony over Wenyuan, but pausing to take her own pulse at Kuqiao's words. The ache still lingered, yet its jagged edge had softened.
Time truly was the finest medicine, sanding down even the sharpest scars; before long, she trusted the pain would fade away for good.
#CH 144
#TITLE: The Zhu and Chen Families Seal the Betrothal
Over the next seven days, Qianli Xun split her hours between writing huaben scripts and drilling her household staff in Qianli Fist.
Meanwhile, bruised from crown to heel and needing two full weeks to heal, Chen Haoyu stayed holed up inside Yongning Residence nursing his wounds.
Figuring Zhu Shuzhen's fiery temper alone would blow the engagement sky-high, he dismissed the whole matter from his mind while recuperating.
Seven days flew by—and true to his word, Chen Jianglin arrived at Zhu Manor with Yao Qingyan, a matchmaker, and a festive procession of lavish betrothal gifts.
Only when Zhu Xiyan roused the household at dawn to deck the halls did Madam Xiang, Zhu Jingyuan, and Chen Wenxin discover the betrothal was real—thrilled, yet nervous all the same.
Zhu Xiyan waved their worries away: "Parents rule marriages—Shuzhen objecting changes nothing."
So the Zhu household rolled out a grand welcome for the Chens, beaming over the generous betrothal gifts.
Out of the Six Rites of marriage, five—proposing, exchanging names, divining auspiciousness, presenting betrothal gifts, and setting the wedding date—were sealed in one sweep, leaving only the final rite of escorting the bride home once spring flowers bloomed next year.
Pining for Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen cooked up an excuse about discussing local affairs with the Prefect—Qiao Mengrong trusted him without a second thought.
Once inside the city gates, however, Shen Jianchen ordered Bai Feng straight to Wangyue Pavilion.
Driving along, Bai Feng wondered silently: "Didn't Your Highness say we were going to Zhu Manor—why Wangyue Pavilion?"
Not that Bai Feng complained! Visiting Kuqiao outside the gate every three or four days never felt enough—having just seen her yesterday, getting an extra visit today was pure gold!
Yet arriving outside Wangyue Pavilion, His Highness neither stepped down nor let Bai Feng summon Kuqiao; after sitting mute for a quarter-hour, he ordered them on to Zhu Manor.
Red lanterns and silk festoons decked Zhu Manor's main gate like a wedding feast.
Puzzled, Shen Jianchen stepped down and walked to the side gate.
Recognizing him, the gatekeeper bowed: "Your Highness picked an awkward day—the Chen and Zhu families are sealing a betrothal right now, so Master may be tied up."
Having no real business anyway—just hoping to glean a scrap of news about Qianli Xun from Zhu Xiyan—Shen Jianchen almost left.
Still, curiosity nudged him: "Which two are getting betrothed?"
"Our Young Lady and Young Master Chen."
Alarm pierced Shen Jianchen: "Youqi Jushi and Chen Haoyu?!"
"Yes, Your Highness."
Shock struck Shen Jianchen cold: no sooner had Qianli Xun broken clean from Wenyuan than the Zhus turned right around and engaged her to Chen Haoyu?!
"Tell the Prefect I'm waiting outside for him."
Startled at how fast Prince Yi's sunny mood had turned stormy, the gatekeeper dared not stall: "Right away, Your Highness—please wait a moment."
Moments later Zhu Xiyan hurried out beaming and bowed low: "Please step inside, Prince Yi! Happy occasion at Zhu Manor today—Your Highness's arrival doubles our joy!"
"With Zhu Manor busy celebrating, I won't intrude. One thing puzzles me, though: why on earth is Lord Prefect marrying your daughter to the Chens? Don't you remember...?"
Knowing Prince Yi meant Chen Haoyu's rumor stunt, Zhu Xiyan explained, "I wanted to keep that scandal quiet and settle it privately—a marriage alliance is the cleanest fix, and the Chen elders think the world of Shuzhen, so we sealed the betrothal today."
"Does Youqi Jushi agree?"
Surprised a royal prince cared whether his daughter liked the match, Zhu Xiyan figured it had to be over Qiao Wenxian.
He had heard street talk that young Qiao looked dead on his feet since getting betrothed to the Miaos—Prince Yi must be worrying this news would crush his buddy further.
"Parents decide marriages, Your Highness—whether Shuzhen likes it doesn't matter. I know Your Highness is close with Young Master Qiao; please advise him to move on now that he's engaged himself."
Not bothering to correct Zhu Xiyan's guess, frustration pushed the truth right past Shen Jianchen's lips: "I had meant to come propose to the Zhu house myself. Looks like I missed my chance."
Thunderstruck, Zhu Xiyan froze—so Prince Yi himself had his eye on Shuzhen?! No wonder he had moved mountains to kill the rumors! Yet the betrothal was already sealed—too late!
"This humble official is mortified! Had I known Your Highness's heart—please forgive me!"
Forcing an easy shrug, Shen Jianchen said, "Don't panic, Lord Prefect—just teasing. Only pointing out Youqi Jushi isn't desperate for suitors; no need to rush her into a match."
At a total loss for words, Zhu Xiyan only stayed bowed low in silence.
"I have errands to run; won't keep you. Hope Youqi Jushi loves her new match." Leaving Zhu Xiyan standing there, Shen Jianchen climbed back into his carriage and rolled off.
Long after the wheels faded, Zhu Xiyan stood stunned—Prince Yi's sharp edge left zero doubt he truly wanted Shuzhen! Zhu Xiyan kicked himself for being blind as a bat!
Even with Princess Jiahe locked as primary wife, entering Prince Yi's Manor as a concubine beat marrying any merchant clan—making the Zhus imperial kin! What a golden chance blown!
Letting out a long sigh, Zhu Xiyan chalked it up to fate—the Chen clan was still a rich prize anyway—and walked back inside to host his in-laws.
Driving away, Bai Feng's head spun: His Highness talking about proposing to the Zhu house had scared him half to death! What if Princess Jiahe ever heard?!
Thank goodness His Highness laughed it off as a joke—still, what a weird, unfunny joke!
"Bai Feng, back to Wangyue Pavilion."
Swinging the reins around, Bai Feng wondered silently: back to Wangyue Pavilion again?! Could His Highness really be in love with Youqi Jushi? He dared not finish that thought.
Pulling up outside Wangyue Pavilion, Shen Jianchen stayed inside and ordered: "Go tell the gate boy I'm not leaving today until I see Youqi Jushi—tell her to come out to my carriage."
Hearing iron in his master's voice, Bai Feng hopped down and delivered the ultimatum to the gate boy.
Sprinting toward the bedchamber courtyard, the gate boy crashed straight into Xiaguo.
"Watch where you're going, idiot—nearly broke my arm!" Xiaguo rubbed her bruised elbow.
"Sorry, Sister Xiaguo! Prince Yi is outside demanding to see Young Lady—gotta report fast!"
"Miss isn't in her room; she's in the back courtyard. Run there."
As the boy sprinted toward the back courtyard, Xiaguo headed toward the front gate—Prince Yi was bound to know how Young Master Qiao was doing; why not ask?
Spotting Xiaguo step out, Bai Feng hurried up: "Where's Kuqiao?"
Xiaguo rolled her eyes: "All you care about is Kuqiao—if I remember right, you two just met yesterday!"
Bai Feng stuck out his tongue and ignored her.
Stepping up to the carriage, Xiaguo bowed: "Prince Yi, my mistress is in the back courtyard training servants in martial arts; she'll be right out."
"Understood."
Working up her nerve, Xiaguo asked, "Since Prince Yi is close with Young Master Qiao, may I ask how he's been lately?"
"Did Youqi Jushi tell you to ask?"
"No—I wanted to ask myself."
"Since you asked, Wenyuan's doing fine—even put a little weight back on."
"Any idea when Young Master Qiao will step out of Qiao Manor?"
"Hard to say—he hasn't come out in ages, maybe just the cold weather."
"Thank you for telling me, Prince Yi."
Right as Xiaguo finished, Qianli Xun walked out with Kuqiao...
Back in the rear yard, Qianli Xun had been drilling her ten students—every one now solid on Qianli Fist fundamentals.
One maid named Hongdou stood out sharpest of all. Qianli Xun planned to train her a bit more, then bring Hongdou along as a personal bodyguard whenever she went out—no more dirtying her own hands on jerks!
Mid-session, the gate boy ran up panting: "Young Lady, Prince Yi is outside the gate swearing he won't leave until he sees you!"
Dreading another encounter with Prince Yi yet knowing he'd camp at her gate all day if ignored, she figured she'd better shoo this nuisance off fast and walked out with Kuqiao.
Bai Feng shamelessly tugged Kuqiao aside right away, Kuqiao tagging along gladly. Ignoring them, Qianli Xun walked straight to the carriage while Xiaguo stepped clear.
"Greetings, Prince Yi. What brings Your Highness to my door?"
Hearing her cool, casual tone—wait, was she actually HAPPY about the betrothal?! Impatience flared in Shen Jianchen: "Climb aboard. I have questions for you."
Refusing to play along, Qianli Xun stood firm: "Whatever Prince Yi has to ask, ask right here—Shuzhen is listening."
"Climb in, or I'll camp outside Wangyue Pavilion pestering you every single day."
What a shameless pest! Seeing no easier way to buy future peace, she climbed inside to hear what on earth he wanted.
Watching her mistress enter Prince Yi's carriage, Xiaguo muttered inside: "So Prince Yi really DOES have a thing for Miss—never noticed before!"
Glancing from Kuqiao laughing with Bai Feng to the cozy carriage, Xiaguo felt left out and bored—better head back indoors to her books and daydream about Young Master Qiao.
Seated inside the carriage, Qianli Xun dropped all ceremony and snapped, "Spit it out fast, Prince Yi! A single man and woman shut inside a carriage together looks awful—my name's bruised enough without you making it worse!"
Nothing Qianli Xun said could ever make Shen Jianchen mad—just having her sit across from him melted every trace of temper.
"Talking so rude to a royal prince—aren't you afraid I'll punish you?"
"When a prince tosses propriety out the window, I return the favor."
"Fine, won't argue with you. Tell me—did you know the Chen and Zhu families just sealed a betrothal? Are you really that desperate to get married?!"
"What are you talking about?! When did the Zhus and Chens seal a betrothal?!" Alarm jolted Qianli Xun upright.
So she truly had no clue! Equal parts relieved and urgent, Shen Jianchen told her: "I just came from Zhu Manor—red lanterns everywhere like a wedding! Asked the gatekeeper and learned the Chens just came to propose, and your wedding date with Young Master Chen is practically set..."
Shooting up to jump out of the carriage, Qianli Xun felt Prince Yi catch her arm: "Where are you going?"
"To stop that betrothal!"
"Going right now won't change a thing—sit down and let's think of a real countermove."
Seeing dead-serious concern on his face and hoping he might actually have a workable fix, she sat back down.
Releasing her arm, Shen Jianchen asked, "You truly refuse to marry Chen Haoyu?"
"Need you even ask?!"
"Then I'll help you."
"How?"
Pausing a tense heartbeat, Shen Jianchen gambled everything: "Agree to be with me, and I'll stop it."
Without a word, Qianli Xun rose again to leave, only for him to catch her wrist once more: "Just agree to enter my manor as a concubine—with my royal status, making both houses cancel this betrothal takes a snap of my fingers!"
Qianli Xun whipped her head back cold: "That's your grand plan?! Might as well have said nothing! I will NEVER be your concubine—and stay out of my business from now on!"
Wrenching her wrist free, she hopped down from the carriage, skipped going back inside, called Kuqiao over, boarded her own Wangyue Pavilion carriage, and raced straight for Zhu Manor.
Left behind, Shen Jianchen felt equal parts relief and hollow ache: glad to his bones she despised marrying Chen Haoyu, crushed that she still shut every door against his heart.
#CH 145
#TITLE: United by a Common Goal
Qianli Xun arrived at Zhu Manor just after the Chen procession departed.
Vaulting from her carriage without a thought for poise, she charged straight through the gate—none of the servants daring to block her since, cast out or not, she was still Master's daughter.
Storming straight into the main hall to find Zhu Xiyan lounging on his couch, she skipped bowing and demanded, "Why did Father seal a betrothal with the Chen family behind my back—without even asking if I agree?!"
Annoyed at her brashness, Zhu Xiyan set down his book: "Why ask you? Both families' elders agreed—all you have to do is wait till spring and climb into the wedding sedan."
"Father pushed me into marrying Lin Jue and look how that ended in divorce—want to push me straight into a second divorce?!"
"When you married Lin Jue I asked your consent and you said yes—nobody foresaw a divorce! Now rumors ruined your name across Lin'an; what fine house would come calling? Having the Chens propose is pure fortune—instead of thanking your lucky stars, you storm in grilling me?!"
"I'd rather stay single till I die than marry Chen Haoyu!"
"Whether you marry—or whom you marry—isn't up to you! The betrothal is sealed, zero room to back out—raising hell won't change a thing!"
"You sealed it, not me! I refuse to marry him—come spring, the only thing Father will deliver to Chen Manor is my corpse!"
*SMACK!* Zhu Xiyan slammed the table so hard his tea bowl flipped, spilling hot tea across the wood.
"Don't threaten me with death! Even dead, your ghost belongs to the Chen family!"
Right then Chen Wenxin and Zhu Jingyuan walked in steadying Madam Xiang; hearing father and daughter roaring at each other silenced them all.
Madam Xiang hurried to tug her daughter aside: "Sit down and speak calmly with your father."
Too furious to sit, Qianli Xun stood rigid, chest heaving.
Refusing to listen to another word against the match, Zhu Xiyan stepped off his couch, told Madam Xiang, "Talk some sense into her," and marched out the door.
Once Father-in-law cleared out, Chen Wenxin dared speak: "Shuzhen, the Chen family is the wealthiest house in Lin'an—marry over and you'll never suffer a day!"
"What kind of talk is that, Sister-in-law—acting like I'm after money?! It's your brother I'm talking about! We mix like oil and fire—why force me to marry him?!" Grabbing Chen Wenxin's hands, Qianli Xun pleaded, "You're the kindest soul, Sister-in-law—please go back to Chen Manor and beg your parents to call off the betrothal!"
Madam Xiang scolded her: "Watch what you say! A woman dropped from a betrothal can never lift her head in public again! Obey your father and marry into the Chen house in peace."
Zhu Jingyuan joined in: "Haoyu's just a bit wild, Shuzhen—once wed he'll settle down, and once you have kids he'll straighten right up to set a good example."
Not a single soul in the room on her side! Dropping her sister-in-law's hands, Qianli Xun glared: "Mother, Sister-in-law—last time I came asking about a Chen betrothal, Mother swore there was nothing and Sister-in-law swore she knew nothing! Were you both in on fooling me?!"
"I really had no clue back then! Only realized today Father-in-law kept Mother in the dark and buried me in copying books so I wouldn't visit Chen Manor and leak it to you! Gotta hand it to Father-in-law—slick move!" Laughing, Chen Wenxin patted her hand: "Relax! Once you marry Haoyu, if he ever dares give you grief, come straight to me and I'll tan his hide for you!"
Scanning all three relatives—every single face beaming over the match—Qianli Xun realized she stood completely alone.
Then Chen Haoyu flashed into mind! He hated this match just as fierce—why else had he sprinted to Wangyue Pavilion to tip her off?! She had misjudged him earlier; time to go hunt him down and get him working on the Chens to break it off!
"Excuse me, Mother, Brother, Sister-in-law—got urgent business." Calling Kuqiao, Qianli Xun bolted straight out.
Watching her daughter rush off, Madam Xiang sighed: "Pray Shuzhen doesn't stir up fresh trouble and settles smoothly into the Chen house so she has a real anchor in life."
"Right you are, Mother! Truth be told, I used to daydream how lovely it'd be if Shuzhen married Haoyu—never dreamed it'd come true! Haha, Haoyu must've saved a kingdom in a past life to land Jiangnan's foremost female ci poet!" Chen Wenxin beamed.
Seeing her daughter-in-law thrilled comforted Madam Xiang too: "The Chens really have the breadth of a grand family—paying zero heed to street gossip."
Zhu Jingyuan smiled: "Once Wenxin married into the Zhu house, she became one of us—nobody knows Shuzhen better than her! Every visit home she sings Shuzhen's praises, so of course the Chens ignore outsiders' chatter."
Pleased with her daughter-in-law all the more, Madam Xiang remarked, "Come to think of it, Shuzhen only grew close to you after losing her memory—you spoke well of her even before that?"
Chen Wenxin explained, "Of course! Before amnesia Shuzhen kept aloof out of sheer poetic pride, yet her heart was pure gold. Mother wouldn't know—once my maid Yuegui slipped and sprained her ankle right in front of Shuzhen. Shuzhen had Kuqiao help Yuegui into her own courtyard, rub medicine on her ankle, and escort her all the way home! Yuegui grew up beside me at Chen Manor, so hearing her praise Shuzhen nonstop won me right over."
Madam Xiang praised warmly: "So that's why! Shuzhen truly struck gold getting a sister-in-law like you."
Rolling along in her carriage, Qianli Xun wondered where to track down Chen Haoyu—hadn't seen hide or hair of him since his warning, not even today on his own betrothal day! Might as well try Yongning Residence first.
Stepping out before Yongning Residence, Qianli Xun sent her driver home—no need waiting when Wangyue Pavilion stood just down the block.
Looking up at the plaque reading "Yongning Residence," she raised her hand to knock just as a servant walked up the lane.
It was Qinggua returning from the apothecary with fresh salves—the physician's ointments were running low and needed restocking.
Who knew what ruthless bastard had pounded his master so hard—a whole week later and barely half healed! At least his face looked normal again, and winter clothes hid the bruises across his torso.
Spotting a gorgeous young lady standing at the gate—worried she might be some romantic fling Young Master picked up outside—Qinggua approached cautious: "Who is Young Lady looking for?"
Seeing packets and bottles of medicine in Qinggua's hands, Qianli Xun wondered who inside was sick—better not be Chen Haoyu!
"Chen Haoyu stays here—I'm looking for him."
Recognition dawned on Qinggua—this beauty was Miss Zhu! "May I ask what business Young Lady has with my master?"
Kuqiao spoke up: "My mistress has something to discuss with your master. If he's in, go announce her."
Qinggua hesitated: "Could Young Lady tell me what it's about so I can report?"
Kuqiao snapped, "Since when does a servant cross-examine guests?! Just go announce her!"
Wincing at how sharp even the maid's tongue was—imagining Miss Zhu had to be twice as fierce, plus she was about to become his future Young Madam whom he dared not cross—Qinggua backed down fast:
"My master is inside—please wait a moment while I announce you."
After days of salves, Chen Haoyu was off bedrest and roaming the yard—still too bruised to visit Chunxiang Brothel, forced to wait in Yongning Residence until every mark faded before resuming his wild streak.
Busy teasing a chirping songbird in its cage outside, he frowned seeing Qinggua hustle in weighed down with medicine packets:
"Buying medicine—what's the rush, racing to reincarnate?"
"Young Master, your future Young Madam is at the gate asking to see you!"
"Future Young Madam who?! Speak plain!"
"Miss Zhu! Today's your betrothal day—doesn't that make her future Young Madam?!"
Realizing today WAS the seventh day, Chen Haoyu swung a sharp kick into Qinggua's shin: "Shut your filthy mouth and bring her in!"
Twisting too hard sent fresh pain stabbing across his bruised ribs; Chen Haoyu clenched his teeth to hide it.
Rubbing his shin, Qinggua barked at a nearby servant: "Deaf?! Go bring her in!"
The servant sprinted off.
Once the throb eased, Qinggua helped his master into a chair: "Sit tight, Young Master—I'll put this medicine away and come right back."
Stepping into the courtyard, Qianli Xun noted Yongning Residence dwarfed Wangyue Pavilion, decked out with lush, pricey flair.
Spotting Zhu Shuzhen walk in, Chen Haoyu leaned back putting on breezy nonchalance: "Well, Zhu Shuzhen—today's betrothal day! Blew the whole thing up already and rushed over to bring me the good news?"
"Hate to disappoint you—I arrived one step too late. Both families already sealed the betrothal."
"WHAT?! Sealed already?! And here I gave you credit thinking you'd nuke it in two seconds—turns out you're useless! Don't tell me you actually WANT to marry me?!"
"Shut your rotten mouth! I'd marry a turnip before marrying you! If you hate it so much, why were you hiding out in Yongning Residence instead of stopping it today?!"
"Long story—got badly injured a few days back and had to hole up here healing! What's YOUR excuse, walking around healthy as an ox?!"
"My dad lied straight to my face swearing there was no betrothal, and I fell for it! Then he sealed it behind my back today! I came to tell you to get back to Chen Manor and talk your family into breaking it off right now!"
"Not worried a broken engagement will smear your name?"
"Hmph! Rumors already dragged my name through every gutter in Lin'an—what's a broken betrothal? Go talk them round! Being laughed at beats marrying you ten times over!"
Her jab stung, yet sharing the exact same wish kept him from snapping back. At mention of the rumors, he probed cautious: "Didn't your dad tell you who started the rumors?"
Suspicion flashed in Qianli Xun's eyes: "Why ask? Don't tell me YOU started them?!"
Aha—Zhu Xiyan hadn't told his daughter Chen Haoyu was the culprit!
Sly old fox—keeping peace with the Chens while offloading his daughter onto him, saddling him with a tigress before he even met a girl he liked!
"Me?! No way! Just saying whoever spread them is pure scum—if you ever caught him, what would you do?"
"Catch him and I'd yank out his tendons, peel off his hide, and beat him flat!" Fire blazed in Qianli Xun as if the rat stood right before her, before cooling down: "Doubt they'll ever catch him anyway, and I don't care anymore since folk stopped talking. Back to business: when are you going home to talk your parents round?"
"For my own future? Heading back right this second!"
Surprised at his swift cooperation, Qianli Xun felt Chen Haoyu wasn't completely hopeless for once, even asking: "How are your injuries holding up?"
"Not fully healed, no big deal. Relax—in a few days you'll get the sweet news that our betrothal is off!" Chen Haoyu promised loud and sure.
Smiling in relief: "Great—I'll wait for your good news at Wangyue Pavilion."
For the very first time, the two arch-enemies spoke without throwing a single punch—united by one shared mission.
#CH 146
#TITLE: Old Madam Xia Decides to Drag It Out
After Qinggua changed his dressings, Chen Haoyu insisted on heading straight back to Chen Manor; Qinggua hitched the carriage and drove him home.
Still sore from healing bruises, Chen Haoyu shed his usual swagger as he walked carefully toward his parents' courtyard—already picturing the sweet domestic scene waiting inside.
Truth be told, Chen Haoyu envied his parents' marriage more than anything, wishing he too might meet one woman to grow old beside—only for Heaven to stick him with Zhu Shuzhen instead!
Sure enough, stepping inside, he found Father and Mother engrossed in a chess game, having eyes only for each other.
Waving a maid silent before she could announce him, Chen Haoyu stood watching with quiet envy—sore bruises making him uncommonly mellow today.
"Every single game you let me win—takes all the fun out!" Yao Qingyan teased, beaming.
Making another generous move, Chen Jianglin smiled: "I don't yield an inch to anyone in this world—only to my wife."
"Well, if you insist, I won't hold back—don't pout when you lose!"
"When have I ever been petty or mad at my wife? Once we finish this game, I'm taking you shopping! Our Chen tailor shop hired a master tailor who's already made two new outfits for you, and our cosmetics shop got fresh stock—packed and waiting for us to pick up."
"Why not have a servant deliver them instead of trekking out ourselves?"
"Where's the fun in delivery? I want to take my wife out for fresh air and show you around the shops myself!"
Father had to be the slickest wife-whisperer alive, Chen Haoyu thought—would he ever meet a woman he wanted to spoil like that?
Dropping her winning stone, Yao Qingyan cheered: "I win!"
Chen Jianglin smiled warm: "Your smile is my win."
Seeing the match conclude, Chen Haoyu stepped forward: "Father, Mother—I need to talk to you."
Glancing up, Chen Jianglin's face snapped stern: "Where did you vanish to carousing these past few days?!"
"Didn't go carousing—just stayed at our east-city house."
"Behaving yourself like that?!" Chen Jianglin pressed hard.
Yao Qingyan nudged him: "He's an engaged man now—don't talk to him like a little boy."
"Fair point, Wife." Softening at once, Chen Jianglin gestured: "Sit down and talk with your mother and me."
Chen Haoyu lowered himself cautiously onto a chair.
Yao Qingyan frowned: "Why do you look off today?"
He'd die before admitting Prince Yi had pounded him flat!
"N-nothing... Heard Father and Mother betrothed me to Miss Zhu. We clash like mortal enemies—marrying her means daily hell! Please go break off this betrothal..."
Sending Qinggua outside first, Chen Jianglin exploded the second the door shut: "Nonsense! How do you break off a sealed betrothal—ruining the girl's name?! You pulled that dirty stunt wrecking her reputation—if you don't marry her, who will?! Blame your own reckless hide!"
Yao Qingyan added, "Miss Zhu is stunning and brilliant—marrying her is three lifetimes of luck! Treasure her and never mention breaking it off again."
With both parents shutting him down, panic spiked in Chen Haoyu: "Father! Mother! I don't want to marry a girl I don't love!"
Yao Qingyan chuckled: "Oh? Got a girl you love already? Bring her home for your father and me to inspect—if she's decent, take her as a concubine!"
Chen Haoyu shook his head: "Not yet. I just can't stand Zhu Shuzhen—we'll never last under one roof!"
Irritation crossed Chen Jianglin's brow: "Love doesn't matter! Your mother and I never even laid eyes on each other before our wedding, and look at us now! Go on—quit pestering us. This betrothal stays."
Seeing zero crack in his parents, he pivoted to Plan B—Grandmother: "Enjoy your chess, Father and Mother—won't bother you anymore."
Noticing his ginger, stiff movements as he rose, Yao Qingyan asked in concern: "Are you hurt somewhere, Haoyu? Why are you moving so carefully?"
A lightbulb flashed over Chen Haoyu's head! "I AM hurt, Father and Mother—Zhu Shuzhen beat me! Found out I spread the rumors, lost her mind, and pounded me black and blue! Hid out at Yongning Residence healing so I wouldn't make you cry—ask Qinggua if you don't believe me!"
Jumping up, Yao Qingyan pulled his collar back to inspect his back—covered in fading purple and yellow bruises! "Heavens above! How did you get beaten like this?! Really by Miss Zhu?! How does a girl hit that hard?!"
Putting on a pitiful whimper, Chen Haoyu lied: "You don't know, Mother—she knows martial arts and hits like a hammer! Marry her and I'll be getting battered by my wife every single day!"
Skeptical, Yao Qingyan shook her head: "I've met Miss Zhu—slender and delicate, nothing like a fighter! Wenxin never mentioned martial arts either. Didn't you get in a street brawl and blame Miss Zhu just to wiggle out of marrying her?!"
Chen Jianglin snorted: "Bet my fortune on it! And even if Miss Zhu DID beat you—bravo to her! Nobody in this house can handle you; a tough wife is just what you need to whip you straight!"
"Father—am I even your real son?!" With that bluff flopping too, panic rose higher.
Yao Qingyan called toward the door: "Qinggua!"
Stepping in at her call, Qinggua received orders: "Walk Young Master back to his room and rub our manor's finest bruise salve over his injuries."
"Right away!" Stepping up to support his master's arm—
Chen Haoyu shrugged him off: "Don't touch me—I can walk! Wait till I marry that shrew, though—I won't even be able to crawl out of bed!"
Once their son stomped out, worry lingered on Yao Qingyan's face: "Husband, should we ask Wenxin back to check? What if Miss Zhu really IS violent and Haoyu suffers?"
Chen Jianglin patted her hand: "Relax, Wife. Look at that brat—lying through his teeth! Picked a fight somewhere else and pinned it on Miss Zhu to kill the betrothal."
Having suspected the same, Yao Qingyan nodded: "True... still, who on earth did he cross to get beaten that bad? I'll go look after him in his room and pry the truth out."
"Go ahead—just looking at that rascal spikes my temper. While you're busy, I'll go talk silk-shop expansion with Jiangshu." Walking his wife to the door, Chen Jianglin headed for his second brother's yard.
Right as Chen Haoyu reached his own courtyard gate, Old Madam Xia arrived tapping her cane, beaming ear to ear: "Heard you came home, Haoyu, so Grandmother rushed straight over! Seven or eight days away and you've lost weight!"
Stepping forward to steady her arm: "Come inside out of the cold, Grandmother."
Supporting her weight through the doorway pulled his sore shoulder, wringing an involuntary hiss between his teeth: "Tss..."
Alarm flashed in Old Madam Xia's eyes: "What's wrong? Where does it hurt? Show Grandmother!"
Time to deploy Grandmother against the betrothal! Putting on his saddest puppy eyes: "Grandmother, do you love me most in the world?"
"Goes without saying!"
"I got beaten black and blue all over—seven whole days later and I'm still sore! That's how brutal it was!"
"What heartless villain touched my grandson?! Tell Grandmother—I'll have your father drag him here and tear him apart!"
Never daring to breathe Prince Yi's name, Chen Haoyu spun his lie: "Nobody else—your future granddaughter-in-law, Miss Zhu!"
Behind them, Qinggua muffled a snicker—Young Master would tell any whopper alive to dodge this wedding!
Baffled, Old Madam Xia frowned: "How could a big strapping lad like you get beaten by a young lady?"
"You don't know, Grandmother—she knows kung fu! Hits like iron; I couldn't even fight back!"
"Why on earth would she beat you?" Old Madam Xia still struggled to picture it.
Unwilling to mention the rumors in front of Qinggua and the maids: "Come inside and I'll tell you, Grandmother."
Catching his master's wink, Qinggua ushered both inside, held every maid outside, and shut the door firm.
Once Old Madam Xia sat, Chen Haoyu spun fast: "Zhu Shuzhen found out I spread rumors about her, went ballistic, dragged me into a dark shack a few days ago, and beat me half to death! Look for yourself, Grandmother..."
Parting his lapel, he revealed his purple-mottled chest, sending horror straight to Old Madam Xia's heart!
"Where does a girl get strength like that?! Beating my grandson like a criminal—how dare we bring her under this roof?! No way—I'm marching straight to talk to your parents!"
Just as Old Madam Xia rose, Yao Qingyan pushed the door open and hurried to steady her elbow: "What brings Mother over here?"
"Can't I visit my own grandson?!" Sitting back down, Old Madam Xia barked, "Good timing—go tell Jianglin Miss Zhu is OUT! Look what she did to my grandson!"
Stepping back to shut the door tight—neither Zhus nor Chens wanted the rumor affair leaking to servants—Yao Qingyan returned beside her mother-in-law: "Like it or not, Mother, he has to marry her—Haoyu brought it on himself pulling that wicked stunt."
"Look at what she did to my boy! Bring her home and she'll flip Chen Manor upside down!" Old Madam Xia wouldn't budge—Haoyu was the apple of her eye, untouchable!
Yao Qingyan knew explaining was useless; Old Madam Xia believed every fairy tale out of her favorite grandson's mouth.
"Wenxin praises Miss Zhu to the skies, Mother—she can't be half as wild as Haoyu claims!"
"Don't care! Look at my grandson's bruises—how could I rest easy?! If we can't break the betrothal, then DRAG IT OUT! Until Miss Zhu tames that temper, no wedding!"
Chen Haoyu pleaded, "I'd still rather break it off, Grandmother—tell Father and Mother to cancel it with the Zhus, please?!"
Old Madam Xia patted his hand: "Grandmother wants to cancel it too, sweetie, yet canceling right after sealing it won't fly—your mother's right, you did mess up and owe amends. Plus she's the Prefect's daughter, not easy to dump overnight. Stalling is our best play right now."
Yao Qingyan fretted: "Stalling won't work either, Mother—New Year is right around the corner, barely two months until the wedding date! How do we stall?"
"Leave that to me—I'll have a plan ready when the time comes." Old Madam Xia looked downright smug.
"Dragging it out will only anger the Prefect, Mother—and we'll still end up marrying her anyway. Better wed on schedule and keep everyone smiling!"
"Quit lecturing me! Haoyu's no weakling, yet a girl beat him raw! One henpecked son letting his wife walk over him is plenty—I refuse to watch a granddaughter-in-law wrap my grandson around her little finger too!"
That jab froze Yao Qingyan silent—how could her mother-in-law constantly preach to her sons to cherish their wives, yet keep sniping that Jianglin spoiled her too much? A mother-in-law's mind was an unsolvable riddle.
"Whatever Mother decides."
After all that acting, scoring only a stalling tactic felt thin to Chen Haoyu, though nothing better existed. Letting out a sigh, he muttered: "Wish I had one more gorgeous older sister!"
Old Madam Xia blinked: "What for?"
"Then His Majesty could take her into the palace, making the Chens imperial kin! Wouldn't have to fear any Prefect—break any engagement we want, marry whoever we please! How awesome would that be?!"
Yao Qingyan tapped his forehead: "Quit talking crazy! Think being imperial relatives is paradise? The Zhus may not dare touch the Qiaos, yet bad blood is already brewed between them."
Tugging her son toward the inner bed: "Go lie down—sent Qinggua for our manor's best salve. Top-notch medicine sitting right at home and you go hiring street doctors!"
Walking inside, she called toward the door: "Qinggua! Get in here and put salve on Young Master!"
Pushing open the door, Qinggua bowed to Old Madam Xia and hurried in to tend his master.
A maid stepped in to escort Old Madam Xia back; satisfied from seeing her boy, she headed off to rest.
#CH 147
#TITLE: Two Plotting, Two Worrying
As Qinggua rubbed ointment across her son's torso, Yao Qingyan saw the full extent of the bruising—fierce enough it was a miracle nothing had broken!
"Tell me the truth, Qinggua—who gave Young Master these bruises?"
Applying salve, Qinggua stammered, unable to meet her eye.
Chen Haoyu jumped in: "Already told you it was Zhu Shuzhen, Mother—why interrogate Qinggua?!"
"Don't buy Zhu Shuzhen for one second! Who did you cross outside? Tell Mother straight and I'll have your father handle it!"
"Believe it or don't—it was Zhu Shuzhen."
Getting nowhere with her stubborn son, Yao Qingyan waited until Qinggua finished dressing him, then pulled the boy aside.
"Tell me who beat Young Master right now, Qinggua, or I'll pack you back to your home village!"
Qinggua hit his knees at once: "Please don't send me home, Madam! Honest, I don't know who beat him either—by the time I tracked Young Master down outside the city, he was already beaten black and blue!"
Hopping off bed, Chen Haoyu protested, "Quit scaring Qinggua, Mother—he really doesn't know."
Relenting, Yao Qingyan told him, "Get up. Watch closer over Young Master from now on and don't let him get hurt again."
Scrambling up relieved, Qinggua's loose tongue slipped: "You didn't see it, Madam—the guy who snatched Young Master moved like lightning! Before I could blink, he yanked Young Master straight into a carriage..."
Chen Haoyu kicked Qinggua's shin: "Get out! Quit making up fairy tales!"
Catching his slip, Qinggua scrambled for the door calling back: "Just joking, Madam!"
Swinging his leg woke a sharp twinge in his ribs, which Chen Haoyu masked fast: "Stop playing detective, Mother—would I lie to you? Didn't Father promise to take you shopping for robes and rouge? Go already before Father gets impatient!"
Taking the clear hint he was shooing her out, Yao Qingyan warned: "Stay indoors healing—no sneaking out, hear me?"
"Heard you, heard you—have fun!" Chen Haoyu nudged her right out the door.

Chen Manor's top-grade salve worked miracles: within three days Chen Haoyu's bruises cleared, his vigor snapped back, and boredom drove him right back out the gate.
Swearing off women for six months had been his revenge vow against Zhu Shuzhen; revenge was over and his own bachelorhood was on the chopping block—zero point keeping celibate now!
Last time he aimed for Chunxiang Brothel, Prince Yi's guard had bagged him right outside the door—rotten luck! Today he'd go celebrate and wash the bad luck off.
First, though, better swing by Wangyue Pavilion to sync plans with Zhu Shuzhen against both their families.
Stepping out Chen Manor's gate with Qinggua, he spotted his usual pack of cronies loitering outside waiting for him.
"Head back indoors, Qinggua—don't tag along."
Qinggua pouted: "Young Master, I want to see Chunxiang Brothel!"
"Thought it over—not your scene. Stay home at Chen Manor and wait on me when I get back."
"Why isn't it my scene? I'm dying to go!"
"Quit arguing—said I'm not taking you! Wanna go, go on your own dime!"
"Chunxiang Brothel costs a fortune—I can't afford that! Give me the silver and I'll go solo!"
Before Chen Haoyu could answer, his cronies swarmed over grinning: "Young Master Chen! Missed you so much we couldn't sleep at night!"
Forgetting Qinggua completely, Chen Haoyu grinned: "Just itching to raise hell on my tab, aren't you?! Good news—my hands are itching too! Come on!"
Watching his master climb into a carriage surrounded by those loud ruffians, Qinggua curled his lip: "Bunch of freeloading street thugs—glad I'm nothing like them! Better off staying cozy inside Chen Manor anyway."
Stopping first at Wangyue Pavilion, Chen Haoyu hopped out with his pack and bumped straight into Huayu at the gate.
"Hey! Aren't you Dongfang Chenze's little sidekick—what's your name?"
Huayu bowed: "Greetings, Young Master Chen. I serve my master—name's Huayu."
"What's got you hanging outside Wangyue Pavilion?"
"Collecting new poems from Youqi Jushi for my master."
"Is Dongfang Chenze loose in the head? Obsessing all day over Zhu Shuzhen's useless scribbles!"
Right then Kuqiao walked out holding folded sheets: "Here you go, Huayu—two brand-new pieces Miss just wrote."
Taking and tucking them safely inside his robe: "Thanks, Sister Kuqiao!"
"Miss also asked you to pass word to Young Master Dongfang—if any poetry salon pops up, invite her! Cooped up in Wangyue Pavilion all day is driving her nuts."
"Rest assured, Sister Kuqiao—I'll tell my master word for word!"
Pleased with his prize, Huayu smiled and hurried off.
Spotting Chen Haoyu and wanting zero part of him, Kuqiao turned back inside—only for Chen Haoyu to yell after her:
"Hey, little maid! Walking right past me without a greeting—no manners! Move it and summon Zhu Shuzhen; I need to talk to her!"
Scowling reluctantly, Kuqiao replied: "Wait here, Young Master Chen."
While waiting, one crony asked, "Here to settle scores with Zhu Shuzhen today, Young Master Chen?"
Chen Haoyu smirked: "You boys need to keep up—haven't heard the news? Zhu Shuzhen and I just got engaged..."
"WHAT?! No way!" Every crony's jaw dropped.
Enjoying their shock, Chen Haoyu chuckled: "Wild, right? Like hell I want her—and she hates it too! So we're two grasshoppers tied to one string now; came over to cook up a plan against our parents."
One crony probed: "Sounds fishy—how on earth did that betrothal happen, Young Master Chen?"
"Buzz off! Above your pay grade—stand back so you don't ruin our strategy talk!"
Knowing better than to cross their meal ticket, the cronies scattered down the lane.
Qianli Xun marched out soon after, demanding loud: "Well, Chen Haoyu—how'd it go?"
Puffing out his chest: "When I step up, consider it almost done!"
Hope flared in Qianli Xun: "Your elders agreed to break off the betrothal?!"
"Well, not break it off..."
"Not break it off—and you're grinning like that?! Come back when they agree!" Spinning on her heel, she started back inside.
Chen Haoyu caught her sleeve: "Hold your horses—haven't finished talking!"
Shaking his hand off: "Hands off! Talk fast—my time is precious!"
"Couldn't get them to cancel it outright, yet I scored real progress: Grandmother ordered them to stall it..."
"Stall it? What's that mean?" Qianli Xun frowned.
"Means when our wedding date rolls up, Grandmother's going to drag her feet and block the ceremony."
"What good does stalling do if I still end up married to you eventually?!"
"Plenty of good! My family stalls from our side while you raise hell from yours—push your dad long enough and the whole betrothal collapses on its own!"
"Me raise hell? How?"
"Got a plan—just swear you won't hit me! Hit me and I walk right now, leaving you stuck married to me in misery!"
Qianli Xun cocked a fist: "Spill it or eat knuckles right now!"
Reaching over, Chen Haoyu gently pushed her raised fist down: "Stop waving fists around—nothing like a noble lady!"
"Get your dirty paw off me and talk!"
Pulling his hand back fast as she lowered her fist, Chen Haoyu began:
"A few days ago I got jumped and beaten half to death..."
Before he finished, Qianli Xun burst out laughing: "Haha! Glad to know someone else in Lin'an hates your guts as much as I do—gotta meet that hero someday!"
"Will you let me finish without interrupting?!" Scowling, he pressed on: "While nursing my bruises, I told my family YOU beat me up..."
Her fist shot straight back toward his nose: "How dare you frame me?!"
Ducking back a step: "How else was I supposed to freak my family out?! That's why Grandmother decided to stall!"
Lowering her fist again: "Since it's for breaking off this wedding, I'll let it slide. Go on!"
"Grandmother swore no wedding until Miss Zhu fixes her violent temper! So all you have to do is stay wild—every time you see me in public, above all in front of your dad, beat me on sight! Betrothal dies guaranteed!"
"Piece of cake! Born with this temper, never changing—and beating you in public is pure pleasure! Just need chances right in front of my dad."
"Leave setting up the stage to me!"
"Since you're playing ball, I'll forgive the framing. Tell me who beat you up, though—I want to shake their hand!"
Refusing to breathe Prince Yi's name: "None of your business! Got real fun waiting—later!"
Waving off, Chen Haoyu rounded up his cronies and strolled off.
"Hmph—keep secrets all you want, I'll find out sooner or later!"
Still, today counted as good news: if the Chens meant to stall, Qianli Xun would happily pile on fuel!
Her mind was set for life now: no romance, no marriage, pour into her passions, cherish close friends, and maybe adopt a child one day if she wanted company in old age.
Meanwhile inside Chen Manor, hearing his cousin Haoyu had gotten betrothed to the Zhus stirred both puzzle and alarm in Chen Haodong, who went straight to his father Chen Jiangshu's room.
Just home from inspecting shops and still waiting on his first sip of tea, Chen Jiangshu looked up: "Free today? Why aren't you at the shop learning the ropes?"
"Studying hard, Father—I'd never slack on an empire this big. Came over because I heard Eldest Uncle betrothed Haoyu to the Zhu family—did Father know?" Standing stiff and humble, Chen Haodong played the model son.
Taking a sip and setting his cup down, Chen Jiangshu nodded: "How could I miss news that big? Doubling our bond with the Zhus—your Eldest Uncle looks thrilled."
"Yet servants saw the Prefect haul Haoyu in bound in ropes with a whip right before the betrothal got sealed—doesn't that smell off, Father?"
"Heard the rumors and wondered too. Probed your Eldest Uncle gently, got dodgy answers, so I dropped it."
"Think about it, Father: with Miss Zhu's reputation dragged through the mud, who else wanted her? Why did Eldest Uncle jump at her? Bet Haoyu screwed up bad, handed the Zhus dirt on him, and forced Eldest Uncle's hand!"
"Why doesn't matter—all that matters is whether Haoyu marrying Miss Zhu helps or hurts US."
"Dead right, Father! On paper, tying closer to the Prefect boosts Haoyu's claim to inherit the Chen empire—yet Miss Zhu's rotten name cuts the other way! Plus I heard Miss Zhu packs a meaner punch than most men and already has bad blood with Haoyu—watch sparks fly if they actually wed!"
"Sharp analysis—my boy's seeing the whole board now!" Beaming, Chen Jiangshu asked, "How'd you learn Miss Zhu and Haoyu have bad blood?"
Chen Haodong smirked: "Father wouldn't know—those freeloaders trailing Haoyu sell their mothers for a coin! Grease a palm and they sing like birds."
"Smart using tools to get intel—didn't waste my lessons on you! Tell me: do you want Haoyu's wedding to happen or blow up?"
Pausing a beat, Chen Haodong answered: "Both dread it and root for it."
"Explain."
"Dread it because marrying the Prefect's daughter might boost Haoyu's standing for the inheritance. Root for it because I'm sick of watching Haoyu live free and wild while still sitting first in line—marry Miss Zhu and he'll get mocked citywide AND battered by his own wife! Makes me grin just picturing it!"
"Been chewing on this myself—bet your Eldest Uncle only agreed because his hand got forced! Dig quiet and confirm why. If his hand really got forced, this match won't add one ounce to Haoyu's standing—might even sink him!"
Chen Haodong bowed: "I'll dig up the whole truth for Father."
"Not for me—for YOU! Make every Chen elder see YOU are the one true heir to the Chen fortune. Start with Haoyu's freeloading pack."
"On it right now, Father!"
Leaving Chen Manor, Chen Haodong stopped by the tea shop for a spell of work, then headed straight for Chunxiang Brothel.
#CH 148
#TITLE: Debating Trade and Planning a Poetry Salon
Waiting outside Chunxiang Brothel, Chen Haodong watched a courtesan emerge moments later, heavy winter silk failing to hide her sultry sway.

Stepping back sharp, Chen Haodong warned, "Not out on a public street, Miss Jinchan—meet me at our usual spot tomorrow at the hour of hai."
Pouting with a playful whine, Jinchan teased, "Always putting on airs, Eldest Young Master—nothing like your cousin! He just dropped a small fortune buying a fresh virgin's first night and is living it up upstairs right now!"

Charging Scarface to keep buttering Chen Haoyu up and bring any fresh intel straight to him, Chen Haodong hurried back to Chen Manor and went straight to his father's courtyard.
Just stepping out to visit his eldest brother about expanding their silk trade, Chen Jiangshu paused as his son blocked the doorway.
"Huge news, Father!"
Turning back inside, Chen Jiangshu shut the door behind his son: "Spill it."
"Guess who started all those rumors about Miss Zhu?"

"Haoyu!"

Bet she only signed off because she knew her darling boy started the rumors and dared not cross the Prefect—and signing off proves even SHE knows Haoyu isn't cut out to lead the clan!"
