Chen Jiangshu mused: "Bold girl, that Miss Zhu—doesn't even care about a maiden's reputation! Fair enough, once mud sticks, what's a little more? Normally a betrothal sealed by both houses never gets undone—still, stay alert. Sit tight and watch from the shadows; if trouble brews, we step in."
"Understood, Father."
"Run along—I'm off to talk silk expansion with Eldest Brother and sound out Haoyu's wedding on the side."
After his son left, Chen Jiangshu walked over to his eldest brother's courtyard.
Inside, Chen Jianglin sat chatting warmly with Yao Qingyan, drawing bright laugh after bright laugh while attending maids looked on in quiet envy.
A maid stepped in to announce: "Master, Second Master is here to see you."
Chen Jianglin groaned playfully: "Can't even chat with my wife without interruptions! Wish I could hand this empire off tomorrow and spend every day strolling and talking with you!"
Smiling warm, Yao Qingyan stood: "You've got big responsibilities—giving me every spare hour is already heaven. Second Brother must have business; I'll leave you two."
Chen Jianglin nodded: "Always so thoughtful! Once Jiangshu and I wrap up, I'm right back to you."
Slipping out the rear door with her maid, Yao Qingyan went for a walk around the manor and a visit with her mother-in-law.
Entering without standing on ceremony, Chen Jiangshu took the chair beside his brother as a maid served tea and withdrew.
Chen Jianglin asked, "Here about expanding our silk shops, Second Brother?"
"Reads minds as ever, Eldest Brother! Right on target." Taking a sip of tea, Chen Jiangshu smiled.
"No mind-reading needed—silk expansion is our top move right now, and you never drop by for small talk, only business! Picked your target markets yet?"
Setting down his tea bowl: "Nobody knows me like Eldest Brother! Yes, mapped it out and came to run it past you."
"Let's hear it."
"We've already covered every corner of the Great Song—what if we push bold, northward and northeast?"
"Into Liao and Jin territory?"
Chen Jiangshu nodded: "Word is folk in both Liao and Jin go crazy for Song silk—open shops up there and profits will soar!"
After a thoughtful pause, Chen Jianglin shook his head: "What built the Chen empire wasn't just deep pockets—it was ONE word: steady. The northern frontier is sitting on a powder keg; open up there and we risk losing our shirts."
Baffled, thinking his brother overcautious, Chen Jiangshu asked, "Why so worried, Eldest Brother?"
"The Great Song just struck the Alliance Conducted at Sea with the Jin to smash the Liao. Once the Jin swallow the Liao, the whole north and northwest will burn with war—no place for Chen shops! Look toward Western Xia and Tubo instead."
Stung at getting shot down yet unable to defy the family head, Chen Jiangshu forced a nod: "Wise as ever, Eldest Brother—we'll go with Western Xia and Tubo."
Business done yet seeing his brother lingering—keeping him from his wife—Chen Jianglin nudged, "Anything else, Second Brother?"
Venturing onto thin ice, Chen Jiangshu asked, "Betrothing Haoyu to Miss Zhu puzzles me, Eldest Brother. With her name dragged through the mud, won't Haoyu marrying her make us a laughingstock?"
"Don't sweat it, Second Brother. Miss Zhu is gorgeous and brilliant—Haoyu's lucky to get her! As for street rumors, I don't buy a syllable. Wenxin raves about her sister-in-law nonstop; if I can't trust my own daughter, who can I trust? Even if outsiders laugh after the wedding, who cares—we live our own lives, not theirs."
Facing his brother's genuine calm, Chen Jiangshu dropped it—he wasn't actually trying to stop it anyway! "Haoyu dug his own grave and Eldest Brother's just covering for him before the Prefect—sit back and enjoy the circus!" he sneered inside.
"Nobody matches Eldest Brother's vision and generosity—lesson taken! Off to plan our silk expansion."
"Go on—keep me posted." Pausing, Chen Jianglin added: "Wait a second."
Halting mid-rise: "Yes, Eldest Brother?"
"Haodong's been shining lately—several clan elders praised him to me. Tell him to drop by and chat with me whenever he's free."
Clear signal Eldest Brother meant to groom Haodong! Elation surged in Chen Jiangshu: "Haodong's the eldest of his generation, duty-bound to set an example for his younger cousins! I'll pass word right away—that boy loves talking shop and family with you."
"Envy you raising a son like that, Second Brother—Haoyu isn't half the man Haodong is! You beat me hands down at raising kids."
"Hardly your fault, Eldest Brother—Mother spoils him rotten; your hands are tied."
"True enough."
Eager to carry the triumph home, Chen Jiangshu bowed: "I'll take my leave, Eldest Brother."
Nodding him out, Chen Jianglin's mind drifted back to his own son's wedding—and Yao Qingyan telling him Mother wanted to stall it! What a stunt!
Unwilling to cross his aging mother directly yet hating the idea of stalling, he had stayed quiet for now—months remained before spring, plenty of time for Old Madam Xia to cool down.
Meanwhile, Shen Jianchen hadn't known a moment's peace since learning of the Zhu-Chen betrothal.
Unable to breathe a word to anyone, agony gnawed at him inside—how could he stand by watching Qianli Xun marry a worthless rake?! Over his dead body!
Yet even a royal prince couldn't smash another family's betrothal without cause—and Qianli Xun wouldn't give him an inch! Had she said yes, he would've used royal power to kill the Chen match and talked Qiao Mengrong into accepting Shuzhen—sure Mengrong would agree.
With Qianli Xun shutting him out cold, he stood cornered—yet refused to sit idle! An idea hit: host a poetry salon!
He laughed at himself—the guy who dreaded poetry salons now throwing one just to see the woman occupying every waking thought! And he'd invite EVERY player on the board!
Strolling over to Qiao Mengrong's courtyard alone—Bai Feng had run off to Wangyue Pavilion again to see Kuqiao, leaving Shen Jianchen green with envy that his own bodyguard strolled to Wangyue Pavilion whenever he pleased while a royal prince stood banned!
Qiao Mengrong beamed seeing him walk in. Lately he visited every day and rarely left Youlin Retreat, proving no other woman waited outside.
Even better, he no longer waited to be announced before entering her bedchamber—proof his amnesiac heart was warming closer to her!
Setting down her brush mid-poem, she smiled: "What does Brother feel like eating today? I'll tell the kitchen."
Skipping the question, he asked, "What are you writing?"
"Just scribbling two lines of verse." Shyly, she hoped he wouldn't ask to read it.
Uninterested, Shen Jianchen said, "Keep at it—let everyone admire your poem at the poetry salon!"
"Brother is hosting a poetry salon?!"
"Day after tomorrow right here at Youlin Retreat—inviting the whole crowd."
"Inviting Shuzhen too?"
"Of course Youqi Jushi! She's Jiangnan's foremost female poet—can't hold a salon without her!"
"Wonderful! I get to see Shuzhen again! Though my little verses are just for myself—no way I'm showing them off in front of Shuzhen!"
Shen Jianchen hesitated a beat: "Afraid Youqi Jushi might turn me down—could you invite her for me?"
Qiao Mengrong smiled with confidence: "Easy! Don't even have to go myself—just send Xilan and she'll say yes on the spot!"
"Deal—Youqi Jushi's on you, I'll handle everyone else."
"Yay! Youlin Retreat hasn't buzzed with life in ages—I'll make the servants prepare everything spotless so Brother is proud!"
Watching Mengrong's sweet, instant devotion stabbed Shen Jianchen with fresh guilt. He vowed to spoil her twice as much for life—even if forced to wed her, he would cherish her like a treasured little sister forever.
#CH 149
#TITLE: Heading to Youlin Retreat for the Poetry Salon
Receiving the invitation card Xilan delivered left Qianli Xun equal parts pleased and puzzled.
Pleased to get an excuse to step out of Wangyue Pavilion, see friendly faces, and breathe fresh air.
Puzzled why on earth Prince Yi was throwing a poetry salon when he barely cared a fig about verse!
Having Kuqiao usher Xilan inside, Qianli Xun asked once the maid stood still, "Who else did Prince Yi invite?"
Xilan listed them: "Young Master Qiao, Lady Miao, Young Master Dongfang, Young Master Cao, Young Master An, and Young Master Chen."
"Why invite Chen Haoyu?! Guy doesn't know one poem from a radish!" She couldn't fathom Prince Yi's angle.
Xilan replied, "Servants don't dare guess His Highness's mind—we just carry out orders."
Hesitation crept into Qianli Xun: Wenyuan AND Miao Zhiyu would both be there, and she wasn't sure her heart was armored enough to face them yet.
Still, turning down an invitation from royalty was rude—and she truly missed her close friend Princess Jiahe.
"Thank Princess for me—tell her I'll be there on time."
Xilan bowed out.
Reading her mistress's face, Kuqiao murmured gentle: "If Miss doesn't feel up to going, just decline—Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe are reasonable and won't hold it against you."
"Sooner or later I have to face the people I dread seeing. Since Prince Yi set the stage, might as well go."
Dropping the subject, Kuqiao went back to grinding ink: "Is Miss's story hitting its finale today?"
Dipping her brush, Qianli Xun smiled: "Yep—wrapping it up today."
"The storyteller asked me to beg for a new story—crowds are hooked and waiting!"
"When you drop off this script, tell him your mistress has plenty more stories up her sleeve—once this wraps, the next one's right behind it."
"Your *Young Master*," Kuqiao corrected with a grin.
"Right, right—Young Master! Look at my slip-up—gotta stay careful not to blow cover!"
Just then Xiaguo stepped in: "Miss, Huayu's outside passing word that Young Master Dongfang is going to Youlin Retreat's poetry salon tomorrow and asks if you're going."
Just asked him to include her in salons and he checked in right away—true friend!
Pausing her brush, Qianli Xun said, "Tell Huayu to let Young Master Dongfang know Prince Yi and Princess invited me too, so I'll definitely be there."
Xiaguo answered "Yes," yet stayed planted by the door.
Kuqiao frowned: "Why aren't you going out to answer him?"
Hesitating a beat, Xiaguo worked up her nerve: "Miss... is Young Master Qiao going?"
Kuqiao snapped, "Xiaguo! How dare you ask Miss that?!"
Pausing her brush without looking up, Qianli Xun answered cool: "Wenyuan was invited too. Tag along tomorrow if you want."
"Thank you, Miss!" Beaming, Xiaguo hurried out to answer Huayu.
Qianli Xun resumed writing as if nothing had ruffled her.
Kuqiao muttered in irritation: "Why let Xiaguo come along, Miss?! She'll just drop to her knees begging Young Master Qiao to make her a concubine again and embarrass you!"
"Everyone has the right to chase happiness. If it makes Xiaguo happy, why fuss over it?"
"Miss is way too good-hearted—just hope Xiaguo snaps out of her delusions."
Finished grinding ink and seeing her mistress deep in focus, Kuqiao moved quietly to the tea table to whisk tea.
...
Next morning dawned bright and clear—a rare crisp blue sky in dead winter.
Weak though winter sunlight felt, just seeing golden rays spill across the earth warmed every soul who stepped outside.
Time had been stitching her heart back together bit by bit, and today's bright sun poured in fresh courage—Qianli Xun felt ready to face Wenyuan and Miao Zhiyu head-on.
Rolling out toward Youlin Retreat, she lifted the carriage curtain to take in the bustling avenue after weeks indoors.
New Year spirit already spiced the air: red spring couplets adorned shops and gates, while bursts of children's firecrackers popped down side lanes.
Another New Year coming—zero excitement for Qianli Xun. Turning nearly twenty-one made her a certified old maid in the Song dynasty!
Funny—no matter which century she lived in, she couldn't shake the "old maid" tag! A wry spark of cheer hit her: maybe from day one of crossing over, she had been walking Qianli Xun's own destiny without realizing it.
Or maybe meeting Wenyuan was the real pivot—why else had a career woman sworn off romance suddenly fallen head over heels, only to rebound right back to her old independent self once love crashed?
Convinced that made total sense, she promised silently: "Fine—then I'll live Qianli Xun's best life right here in the Song!"
Then the Chen betrothal poked back into mind: "Wait—is THAT part of my fate too?" With zero clue how to untangle it, she'd take it one step at a time.
And Prince Yi—throwing a random poetry salon stuffing every awkward romantic rival under one roof! What game was that slick, scheming heartbreaker playing? Locking her shields tight, she swore not to fall into whatever trap he had dug.
Arriving at Youlin Retreat, Qianli Xun discovered she was dead last—thinking she'd left plenty early, yet everyone else had beaten her!
The salon was set in Qiao Wenxian's old courtyard, complete with its outdoor wooden viewing platform for gazing over the winter woods between verses.
Up at dawn, Shen Jianchen had ordered servants to scrub the courtyard and hall spotless.
Tables and chairs stood arranged neat around three roaring braziers pre-heating the room so no guest felt a chill.
For his very first salon at Youlin Retreat, Qiao Mengrong pitched in full tilt—writing out the kitchen menu for Xilan to deliver, and having maids lay fruit, candied preserves, osmanthus wine, and prime tea at every place setting.
Qiao Wenxian had arrived first of all—driven by one sole wish, to lay eyes on Shuzhen a minute sooner. He had put a bit of flesh back on and looked steadier, yet the wound still bled inside.
That hasty breakup in the snow beneath the willow left too much unsaid; he needed one more word with Shuzhen before his heart could truly die.
Hearing she was coming today, he had woken at dawn and tried on half his wardrobe before choosing the exact robe from their very first West Lake date—hoping a shred of that old magic lingered in its silk.
Because it was a thin summer gown, he had layered heavy winter clothes underneath and worn the summer robe right on top.
Shen Jianchen sat with Wenyuan first, reading in his restless fidgeting that he still hadn't let Youqi Jushi go.
Next came Miao Zhiyu—her first time seeing Young Master Qiao since their betrothal, hoping he might treat her warmer now.
As Miao Zhiyu walked in with her maid to bow to Prince Yi, awkwardness froze Qiao Wenxian; only when she greeted him with polite grace—"Young Master Qiao"—did he offer a silent smile without speaking.
Miao Zhiyu deliberately chose the seat right beside him.
Shen Jianchen hid a smirk—today's salon was going to be pure fireworks!
Not that he was here for cheap drama: he just wanted another hour near Qianli Xun, to size up Chen Haoyu's and Wenyuan's real feelings for her—and above all, catch Qianli Xun alone to reveal he wasn't Zhao Pu, he was Shen Jianchen!
Once she knew he was Shen Jianchen, maybe their shared journey across time would move her heart toward him.
Third through the door came Chen Haoyu—baffled why Prince Yi invited a poetry illiterate! Since Bai Feng—the exact guy who pounded him flat—delivered the card, he dared not ask and said yes fast.
Terrified of crossing Prince Yi again, he showed up super early with Qinggua, bowed low to Prince Yi, and tucked himself into the lowest seat directly across from Qiao Wenxian.
Knowing his own limits, keeping low beat making a fool of himself over poetry. Spotting Qiao Wenxian and Miao Zhiyu opposite him, he tossed out a cheeky greeting: "Send me an invite when you two tie the knot—I'll bring a massive gift!"
Miao Zhiyu flushed crimson, too shy to reply.
Mind stuck on Shuzhen, Qiao Wenxian ignored him cold.
Holding back a laugh, Shen Jianchen decided to deal Wenyuan a reality check right now: "Quit teasing Wenyuan, Young Master Chen—heard you're getting married soon too! When's the big day?"
Unable to dodge Prince Yi's question, Chen Haoyu answered honest: "Hate this match—whether it ever happens is anyone's guess."
Pleased—the last thing Shen Jianchen wanted was another man pining over Qianli Xun—he remarked, "Sounds like Young Master Chen really dislikes this betrothal."
"Sigh—Qiao Wenxian and I are two poor saps! The one he wants, I wouldn't touch with a pole—tell me, Prince Yi, why does neither of us get what we want?"
Before Prince Yi could reply, alarm snapped Qiao Wenxian upright: "What did you just say, Chen Haoyu?! What 'one I want' won't you touch?!"
"Whoa, didn't you hear? Let me fill you in: the Zhus and Chens sealed a betrothal—my bride-to-be is your precious Zhu Shuzhen! And I want zero part of marrying her! Honestly, how spineless are you—if you'd married her, I wouldn't be stuck with this betrothal!"
Fury surged in Qiao Wenxian to jump across the table and smash Chen Haoyu's face—why on earth was his beloved Shuzhen promised to this clown?! Then helplessness dragged him back down; blame cruel fate, not him. Clamping his jaw, he sank into dead silence.
Quiet joy warmed Miao Zhiyu—now that Youqi Jushi had a new match, marrying Young Master Qiao carried less guilt! Speaking up smooth: "Linking the Chen and Zhu houses doubles a fine bond—marrying Youqi Jushi is a rare blessing for Young Master Chen."
Chen Haoyu snorted: "Easy for Miss Miao to say, marrying your dream man! How could you feel my pain? Not only am I Lin Jue's fallback guy, I'm signing up for daily wife-beatings from Zhu Shuzhen—isn't my life tragic?!"
With nothing polite left to say, Miao Zhiyu fell quiet, casting sidelong glances at silent Qiao Wenxian, her heart fluttering toward their spring wedding.
Irritated at Chen Haoyu's disrespect toward Qianli Xun, Shen Jianchen dropped a pointed barb: "Blame those rumors breaking out right on cue to ruin everyone's happiness—don't you agree, Young Master Chen?"
Feeling the sting of Prince Yi's warning, Chen Haoyu froze with a sheepish grin, tasting sweet karma straight in his teeth.
Saved by the bell—the three young masters walked in right on time, pulling eyes off his red face!
Bumping into each other outside town, all three had ridden in together—arriving extra early to show utmost respect for Prince Yi's invitation.
Seeing three guests already seated alongside Prince Yi in the host's chair, they hurried forward bowing: "Greetings, Prince Yi!"
Shen Jianchen nodded warm: "Sit wherever you like—no strict seating rank today."
Dongfang Chenze slid into the seat beside Chen Haoyu, while Cao Ban and An Mingxuan took the two seats next to Miao Zhiyu.
Standing behind his master, Huayu winked at Nan Shuang behind Young Master Qiao, who gave a subtle wave back.
Noticing Chen Haoyu, Huayu puzzled why a rake was invited to a poetry salon—then sized up Qinggua behind him, spotting zero scholarly polish and pegging him a total blockhead with quiet disdain.
Once seated, Dongfang Chenze spoke for the trio: "We tried arriving early, Prince Yi—didn't expect half the room to beat us! Forgive us if we looked slack."
"Nonsense, Young Master Dongfang—right on time, neither early nor late! Others haven't even arrived yet, so let's wait in comfort."
Meaning Princess Jiahe and Youqi Jushi—Princess lived right here and could pop over in a breath, so everyone silently hoped Youqi Jushi wouldn't show up too late.
After trading nods around the hall—all three privately baffled at Chen Haoyu's presence yet mute before royalty—everyone sat waiting in hushed anticipation.
#CH 150
#TITLE: Tangled Hearts Under One Roof
Maids filed in setting out fresh fruit and tea, pouring a cup for every guest before withdrawing in silence.
Barely had everyone taken a sip when Princess Jiahe arrived with Xilan. Once guests finished greeting her, Qiao Mengrong asked, "Brother, the kitchen's feast is ready—dine first, or appraise poetry first?"
Never caring a whit about poetry in the first place, Shen Jianchen announced, "It's mealtime already. Once everyone's here, we eat first with music and dance, then listen to storytelling, and save poetry appraisal for last."
Every guest except Chen Haoyu blinked in disbelief—a poetry salon where poetry got shoved to the dessert menu?! Before Prince Yi, nobody dared peep, nodding along.
Chen Haoyu lit up—NOW he got why Prince Yi invited him! Feast first, listen to storytelling, then cook up an excuse to duck out before poetry started—zero awkwardness!
"Right away!" Qiao Mengrong instructed Xilan: "Tell the kitchen to bring out wine and dishes the second everyone is seated."
Xilan hurried off.
Taking the table directly across from Qiao Wenxian, Qiao Mengrong scanned the hall—only Shuzhen remained.
Everyone had turned up remarkably early today—even Prince Yi himself! Normally a royal host arrived dead last, yet he had been sitting right here waiting since Young Master Qiao stepped in.
If not for Young Master Qiao keeping him company, Qiao Mengrong might almost have wondered if he was eager to see another guest.
Right on cue, Shuzhen walked through the doorway with both maids trailing behind...
Every pair of eyes swiveled toward Youqi Jushi.
Qiao Mengrong smiled seeing bright color back in Shuzhen's cheeks—clearly she had stepped out of the worst of her heartbreak.
Glancing across at Young Master Qiao, she saw his eyes flare bright at Shuzhen's entrance, shadowed by raw sorrow.
Glancing next at Miao Zhiyu, she watched her look from Shuzhen to Qiao Wenxian beside her, then lower her eyes and lift her tea bowl to hide behind a sip.
Happy as Qiao Mengrong was about the salon, she couldn't fathom why Prince Yi had packed every tangled love rival into one room—he had never cared for cheap gossip, before amnesia or after.
So busy reading everyone else's faces, she missed her own fiancé's eyes—shining no less ardent than Qiao Wenxian's the instant Youqi Jushi walked in!
Only when Qianli Xun stepped forward bowing—"Greetings, Prince Yi, Princess Jiahe"—did Shen Jianchen catch himself and pull his gaze back lest the room notice.
"Dead last to arrive, Youqi Jushi—bigger airs than a royal prince! Since this is a poetry salon, your penalty is composing a fresh ci on the spot for everyone!" Shen Jianchen teased harsh on purpose just to trade an extra sentence with her.
"Last to arrive doesn't mean late, Prince Yi—no grounds to penalize me! Still, at a poetry salon composing a ci is only proper; no need to dress it up as punishment."
Qianli Xun fumed inside: "Not my fault you camped out at dawn! Everyone else kissed up to a prince showing up early—now you pick on me in public?!"
Watching Prince Yi needle Zhu Shuzhen delighted Chen Haoyu, though he had to admire her nerve talking back to royalty—making him even surer she was poison to marry! Imagine getting roasted like that by your own wife every day!
To Chen Haoyu's shock, Prince Yi didn't flare up one bit—inviting Zhu Shuzhen to sit and beaming that he looked forward to her ci! Night and day from the prince who had him beaten black and blue!
Directed to take a seat, Qianli Xun found only one spot left—sandwiched between Dongfang Chenze and Mengrong—and settled in.
Trading a warm smile with Mengrong, she glanced across to see Miao Zhiyu, the bride taking her place beside Wenyuan, and offered a courteous smile.
Very different from the smile she gave Mengrong—carefully polished to look effortless, unbothered, free of awkwardness.
Miao Zhiyu returned a bright smile—winning Wenyuan had her glowing!
Then came the gaze Qianli Xun dreaded yet couldn't dodge: Wenyuan staring straight through her, eyes drenched in agony and longing.
Afraid looking one second longer would reignite old ashes, Qianli Xun dropped her eyes straight onto her plate of fruit.
Sensing tension thickening the room, Qiao Mengrong nudged, "Everyone's here, Brother—time to serve lunch."
Lost in a daze, Prince Yi didn't even hear her.
Following his locked gaze straight to Shuzhen—assuming he was gauging Shuzhen's mood on Young Master Qiao's behalf—Qiao Mengrong called louder: "Brother!"
Snapping awake, Shen Jianchen tore his eyes off Qianli Xun: "Oh—what is it?"
Qiao Mengrong repeated: "Everyone is here."
"Serve the feast, then!"
Servants stationed outside heard the command and sprinted to the kitchen; moments later maids streamed in laying wine and dishes before every guest.
Standing behind her mistress during the service, Xiaguo kept her eyes glued to Young Master Qiao—he'd gained a little weight, yet one look told her he still hadn't let Miss go.
Watching Xiaguo stare nonstop at his master without sparing him half a glance burned Nan Shuang raw.
Glancing over at Xiaguo, Kuqiao frowned too—sure enough, eyes glued straight on Young Master Qiao! Born a maid, Nan Shuang was already a fine match; chasing Young Master Qiao was sheer folly.
Not like Kuqiao, happy with her own match—glancing toward Prince Yi's elbow, she caught Bai Feng looking right back at her!
Sensing the electric awkwardness humming across the hall, the three young masters stayed mute, figuring they'd enjoy the feast and wait until poetry time to speak up.
Once every dish stood set, Shen Jianchen invited: "Dig in, everyone—no standing on ceremony!" Then he nodded toward Bai Feng.
Clapping his hands, Bai Feng signaled dancers to glide out from both wings as music struck up, slender waists swaying in graceful rhythm.
Qianli Xun wondered if Prince Yi even knew what a poetry salon was—zero poetry, nonstop food and dancing! Why not just call it a banquet?!
After eating a spell and watching a dance, eager to escape Wenyuan's burning stare for fresh air, Qianli Xun leaned close to Mengrong: "Stepping out to freshen up."
Qiao Mengrong whispered back: "Don't take long."
Nodding, Qianli Xun rose and headed out, Kuqiao hurrying right behind to drape her winter cape over her shoulders, while Xiaguo stayed planted—no way she was walking away from Young Master Qiao! Leaving Xiaguo behind, Qianli Xun slipped out the rear door with Kuqiao.
Stepping into crisp outdoor air cleared the heaviness from her lungs. Bare winter branches stretched stark over Youlin Retreat, lending bleak charm to the grounds.
With leaves stripped away, pale winter sunlight poured freely down onto her shoulders, cleaner and gentler than indoor braziers.
Strolling unhurried along the garden path enjoying the sun, a cluster of blooming winter plum trees caught her eye—a rare splash of life amid the bare woods.
Delighted, she walked closer and leaned in to breathe the crisp, cool plum fragrance, sharp enough to clear her mind.
She had fooled herself thinking she was over Wenyuan; one glimpse today proved letting go wasn't that simple—still, she had to keep acting over him. What else could she do?
Just needed more time—by spring, or summer, or a year down the line, she'd get past him for good.
Having drunk in the plum scent, she strolled further down the path while Kuqiao trailed quiet behind...
Back indoors, watching Shuzhen step out, Qiao Wenxian rose and headed outside too. Tension gripped Shen Jianchen, though he forced an easy mask, keeping his eyes on the food and dancers.
Chen Haoyu smirked inside: "Go on—rekindle that flame and cause a scandal so I get a free ticket out of this wedding! Heheh..." Catching sight of Miao Zhiyu's downcast face, he lifted his cup in a cheeky silent toast.
Ignoring Chen Haoyu's antics, Miao Zhiyu stewed in humiliation: engaged to her, yet Young Master Qiao hadn't spoken one word to her, kept staring at Youqi Jushi, and chased straight outside the second she left! Where did that leave her pride?!
The three young masters traded anxious looks, praying silently no disaster broke out.
The instant Young Master Qiao stepped out, Xiaguo saw no point standing indoors and slipped after him. Catching up where nobody else stood around, she called bold: "Young Master Qiao!"
Halting, Qiao Wenxian turned around with Nan Shuang, who snapped, "What do you want with my master?"
Ignoring Nan Shuang completely, Xiaguo stepped close: "You're an engaged man now, Young Master Qiao—stop clinging to my mistress! Miss has already let you go completely; stop bringing her trouble!"
"Bringing her trouble? Did Shuzhen tell you to say that?" Pain twisted Qiao Wenxian's face.
Xiaguo pressed firm: "I'm beside Miss every hour—I know every thought in her head! Your fiancée is sitting right inside while you chase after my mistress—did you spare one thought for how Miss Miao feels?!"
"I don't care about any of that—I just need to speak with Shuzhen!" Turning sharp, Qiao Wenxian started back down the path.
Darting ahead to block him, Xiaguo cried, "Wake up, Young Master Qiao! You and Miss are impossible—let her go! Let go, and both you and Miss get peace!"
"Let go? Think it's that easy?! Step aside!"
Out of ammo, Xiaguo dropped her trump card: "Maybe Young Master Qiao hasn't heard—Miss is already betrothed to Young Master Chen!"
"I already know! So what—aren't I betrothed too?!"
Seeing truth fail, Xiaguo spun a brazen lie: "What Young Master Qiao doesn't know is Miss told her sister-in-law she's already fallen in love with Young Master Chen and is marrying into the Chen house with all her heart..."
A thunderbolt out of a clear sky! Forgetting all about chasing Shuzhen, Qiao Wenxian spun round and stormed straight back into the hall.
Drowned out by music and dancing, nobody noticed his fury—until he snatched a tea bowl off the table and flung its contents straight into Chen Haoyu's face! Gasps froze the entire hall.
At a wave from Shen Jianchen, the music died and the dancers scurried out.
Drenched out of nowhere, Chen Haoyu exploded: "What the hell's gotten into you, Qiao Wenxian?! Splashing tea in my face?!"
Seizing Chen Haoyu by the collar, Qiao Wenxian roared: "Who gave you the right to get betrothed to Shuzhen?!"
Shocked murmurs rippled through the three young masters—Youqi Jushi betrothed to a notorious rake?! Unthinkable!
"Weird—knew about it ten minutes ago and didn't touch me, step outside two minutes and now you go feral?!" Furious himself and itching to twist the knife, Chen Haoyu sneered: "You're engaged to Miao Zhiyu—what right do YOU have meddling with my Shuzhen?! Fighting just made Shuzhen and me fall harder for each other! Seeing you two flop, I begged my dad to propose to the Zhus—and boom, bull's-eye! The Prefect jumped at it and called me the son-in-law of his dreams!"
Shen Jianchen sneered inside: "Shameless dandy—lying through his teeth!"
Snapping past reason, Qiao Wenxian cocked a fist to swing, only for Chen Haoyu to catch his wrist cold—stronger by far, locking Qiao Wenxian's punch dead in mid-air.
Unable to sit by, Shen Jianchen barked loud: "Wenyuan is my brother—show some restraint, Young Master Chen!"
At Prince Yi's command, Chen Haoyu couldn't push further and shoved Qiao Wenxian back: "For Prince Yi's sake I'll let this slide. Just remember: Zhu Shuzhen belongs to ME now, so keep your eyes off her! Your own fiancée is sitting right there—acting like this only hurts her!"
Staggering back into Nan Shuang's arms, Qiao Wenxian couldn't brawl before Prince Yi. Frozen where he stood, Chen Haoyu's taunt—"Shuzhen belongs to me now, keep your eyes off her"—hammered through his skull, wiping out every earlier complaint Chen Haoyu had made about hating the match until the whole world drained gray...
Rising from her chair, Miao Zhiyu stepped beside Qiao Wenxian and faced Chen Haoyu with calm poise: "Young Master Chen is mistaken. Young Master Qiao's feelings don't hurt me at all—quite the opposite, I understand completely. I know every chapter of Youqi Jushi and Young Master Qiao's bond and wished they could end up together; only because our families sealed this betrothal were our hands tied."
Her generous, gracious speech drew admiring glances from every corner of the room—even Qiao Wenxian looked at her with fresh respect, a quiet wave of guilt stirring in his chest.
Watching from the side, Xiaguo beamed inside: reasonable and gracious—Miao Zhiyu would make an easy primary wife to live under once Xiaguo became Young Master Qiao's concubine!
Knowing she had won every heart in the hall, Miao Zhiyu turned gently to Qiao Wenxian: "Sit back down and finish lunch, Young Master Qiao. Youqi Jushi only stepped out to freshen up and will be right back—whatever you need to tell her, tell her once she returns."
Backed gently into grace by Miao Zhiyu, Qiao Wenxian sank back into his chair—a thousand words that had burned to burst out at Shuzhen now caught silent in his throat.

## 译法建议
牛郎 | the Cowherd (niulang) | 第101章千里寻将古代神话“牛郎”与现代俚语“男公关/陪酒男”对比，首现内嵌拼音与现代语义反差（male escort），保住原作穿越笑点。
《中秋》 / 《中秋玩月》 | "Mid-Autumn" / "Enjoying the Moon at Mid-Autumn" | 第125章朱淑真两首五律未见权威英译本，自译并标 [TN: Translator's rendering]，且严格对应第126章文人对“寒”“老”二字的品评。
不识庐山真面目，只缘身在此山中 | "Why can't I tell the true shape of Lu-shan? Because I myself am in the mountain." | 第122章直接采用汉学家 Burton Watson 权威译本并标 [TN: Translated by Burton Watson]。
邂逅相遇，适我愿兮 | "We met together accidentally, and so my desire was satisfied" | 第130章颜池所引《诗经·野有蔓草》采用 James Legge 权威译本并标 [TN: Translated by James Legge]。
颜池 / 延迟 | Yan Chi (sounds like yanchi, "delayed") | 第130章保留千里寻谐音调侃梗，内嵌拼音与词义解释（合乎规则一.4）。
居养院 / 安济坊 / 漏泽园 | poorhouses / charity infirmaries / pauper cemeteries | 第127章北宋崇宁/宣和年间官办福利机构，意译清晰准确，免去冗长音译拖累对话节奏。
海上之盟 | the Alliance Conducted at Sea | 第148章采用国际汉学界标准史学译名。
吐蕃 | Tubo | 第148章采用宋代历史语境准确音译，避免直接以现代 Tibet 置换。
青瓜 | Qinggua ("Cucumber") | 第113章首现交代得名由来时内嵌词义，后续直用音译 Qinggua。
疤子 | Scarface | 第148章“脸上有刀疤的小弟”绰号，意译 Scarface 更符合英文网文江湖画面感。
六礼（纳彩、问名、纳吉、纳征、请期、亲迎） | the Six Rites of marriage (proposing, exchanging names, divining auspiciousness, presenting betrothal gifts, setting the wedding date, escorting the bride home) | 第144章功能意译，英文读者一目了然且完整保留宋代婚俗制度层次。
自审说明 | 101–150章共50章3856段全齐，沿用91–100章已纠偏术语（Youlin Retreat / Qiao Manor / Juxiang Tower / Dongpo pork / tigress / shrew / Hongxiang & Xuanying 等），并已过滤动作动词-ly副词、AI高频词与防误读亲属称谓；第104–106章及128章“焚烧手稿”均严格忠实原文，未补写“断肠集”等原文外史实。 | 整批初译+自审一次合并成终稿。
